@echo off
setlocal DisableDelayedExpansion
title AeroKinetic - build PRIVATE portable Windows application
rem Save as BUILD_AEROKINETIC_UI_050.bat next to 6_run_AEROKINETIC.bat.
cd /d "%~dp0"
if errorlevel 1 exit /b 1
if not exist ".venv\Scripts\python.exe" (
  echo Put this BAT in your EXISTING AeroKinetic folder on the HOME Windows PC.
  echo This folder must contain .venv and 6_run_AEROKINETIC.bat.
  pause
  exit /b 1
)
set "AK_BUILD_SELF=%~f0"
set "AK_BUILD_ROOT=%~dp0."
set "AK_BUILD_PYTHON=%~dp0.venv\Scripts\python.exe"
echo Run on the HOME Windows PC with internet and working C++ calculation tools.
echo Close AeroKinetic. Administrator rights are not needed for this build.
echo The existing app and its .venv will not be changed.
echo A private compiler/SDK copy and isolated environment will need several GB.
echo Preparation and cold-cache tests can take several minutes. Keep this window open.
powershell.exe -NoLogo -NoProfile -NonInteractive -Command "$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'; $code=1; try { $raw=[IO.File]::ReadAllText($env:AK_BUILD_SELF); $m=[regex]::Match($raw,'(?ms)^AK_BUILDER_BEGIN\r?\n(?<data>.*?)^AK_BUILDER_END\r?$'); if (-not $m.Success) { throw 'Save the complete TXT, including the payload.' }; $bytes=[Convert]::FromBase64String($m.Groups['data'].Value); if ($bytes.Length -ne 684899) { throw 'Incomplete build kit.' }; $sha=[Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create(); try { $hash=([BitConverter]::ToString($sha.ComputeHash($bytes))).Replace('-','').ToLowerInvariant() } finally { $sha.Dispose() }; if ($hash -ne '0c1f8468b2c7cfab1cc4288a674fa8df95eb7524dc15b82a8fd28f886dd13979') { throw 'Build kit SHA-256 mismatch. Save the complete TXT again.' }; $folder=Join-Path (Join-Path $env:AK_BUILD_ROOT 'portable_builds') ('kit_'+[Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')); New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $folder -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null; $zip=Join-Path $folder 'builder.zip'; [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($zip,$bytes); Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $zip -DestinationPath $folder -ErrorAction Stop; $manifest=Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $folder 'KIT_SHA256.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json; foreach ($entry in $manifest.PSObject.Properties) { $file=Join-Path $folder $entry.Name; if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $file -PathType Leaf)) { throw ('Missing kit file: '+$entry.Name) }; if ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $file).Length -ne $entry.Value.bytes -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $file -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() -ne $entry.Value.sha256) { throw ('Kit file differs: '+$entry.Name) }; }; & $env:AK_BUILD_PYTHON (Join-Path $folder 'tools/portable/build.py') --app-root $env:AK_BUILD_ROOT --kit-root $folder; $code=$LASTEXITCODE; if ($code -ne 0) { Write-Host 'Build failed. Send PORTABLE_BUILD_LAST.log from the original app folder.' -ForegroundColor Red }; } catch { Write-Host $_.Exception.Message -ForegroundColor Red; $code=1 }; exit $code;"
set "AK_BUILD_RESULT=%ERRORLEVEL%"
echo BUILD_EXIT_CODE=%AK_BUILD_RESULT%
if "%AK_BUILD_RESULT%"=="0" echo Open PORTABLE_READY_TO_COPY.txt for the exact folder to transfer.
pause
exit /b %AK_BUILD_RESULT%
